Actually a cleaner standard approach: define LR as SN plus extra, and prove "CR" properties: if R_T(t), then SN t. Then define arrow LR as `SN(t) /\ forall u, value u -> R_{T1}(u) -> R_{T2}(app t u)`. This is the usual "CR1/CR2/CR3" approach for call-by-name; for CBV there are some subtleties.

Alternative simpler approach for STLC CBV: since the language is simply typed and strongly normalizing, we could prove SN directly by a different method: e.g., reducibility candidates via "hereditarily strongly normalizing values" and a substitution lemma. But actually, maybe simpler: use the standard "logical relation" with the "neutral terms" trick? Hmm.

Hmm, but maybe an even simpler approach exploits call-by-value: define measure via "size of normal forms"? That's complicated when applications have large arguments.

Let me think about the standard approach with the logical relation defined as:

```
Fixpoint R (T : ty) : tm -> Prop :=
```

```
Fixpoint subst (f : atom -> tm) (t : tm) : tm :=

  | tm_bvar i => tm_bvar i
```

Design of the logical relation:

```
Fixpoint R (T : ty) : tm -> Prop :=
  match T with
  | Ty_Bool => fun t => strongly_normalizing t
  | Ty_Arrow T1 T2 => fun t =>
      strongly_normalizing t /\
      (forall v, value v -> R T1 v -> R T2 (tm_app t v))
  end.
```

Now, the remaining major piece: the application case of the fundamental theorem:

T_App: Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 with has_type Gamma t1 (T1 -> T2), has_type Gamma t2 T1. IH gives R (T1->T2) (subst f t1) and R T1 (subst f t2). Need R T2 (subst f (tm_app t1 t2)) = R T2 (tm_app (subst f t1) (subst f t2)).

Let a = subst f t1, b = subst f t2. We have R_arrow a: SN a and forall v value, R T1 v -> R T2 (app a v). We have R T1 b (hence SN b).

Goal: R T2 (app a b).

This is the classic hard part. Standard solution: prove a lemma:

`Lemma R_app : forall T1 T2 (t1 t2 : tm), R (Ty_Arrow T1 T2) t1 -> R T1 t2 -> R T2 (tm_app t1 t2)`.

- P1(T): forall t, R T t -> SN t.

Similarly, R_step (backward closure) global lemma: 
```
Lemma R_step : forall T t t', R T t' -> step t t' -> R T t.
Proof.

  - ~ is_abs t: intro Habs; destruct as [T' [body Heq]]; subst; apply abs_no_step in Hstep; contradiction.
  - SN t: apply SN_intro; intros t'' Hstep''; rewrite (step_det _ _ _ Hstep Hstep''); apply R_SN; auto.
  - intros t'' Hstep''; rewrite (step_det _ _ _ Hstep Hstep''); auto.
Qed.
```

For CBV, the `size` (number of nodes) decreases? β: (λx.body) v → body[v/x]; size(body[v/x]) can be larger than size(body) + size(v) + 1? E.g., body uses x multiple times: λx. x x applied to v: body[v/x] = v v, size 2|v|+1 vs original |body|+|v|+1 = 3+|v|. If |v| large, e.g. |v| = 10, then 21 > 13. So no.

The standard simple measure: define for a typed term a "complexity" as the sum over applications of something like (max reduction length), defined recursively via the logical relation. That's the logical relation again.

So logical relation it is. Let's get precise and write the Coq code.

So define `fv : tm -> list atom`? Or a predicate `appears_free : atom -> tm -> Prop` with a lemma that for any finite list L there's an atom not free in t and not in L. Using a list of free vars is convenient:

```
Fixpoint fv (t : tm) : list atom :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar _ => []
  | tm_fvar x => [x]
  | tm_app t1 t2 => fv t1 ++ fv t2
  | tm_abs _ t1 => fv t1
  | _ => []
  end.
```
Then `subst_fv_eq : ~ In y (fv t) -> forall f v, subst (extend f y v) t = subst f t`. Prove by induction on t. For the abs case, need fv body; straightforward.

Hmm, careful with `extend`: define `extend (f : atom -> tm) (x : atom) (v : tm) : atom -> tm := fun y => if Nat.eqb x y then v else f y`. Then `(extend f x v) x = v` since eqb x x = true. And `x ≠ y -> (extend f x v) y = f y`.

For the context update: `update Gamma x T y = if Nat.eqb x y then Some T else Gamma y`. Same structure ✓.

Fresh-picking: define `fresh (L : list atom) : atom` such that ~In (fresh L) L. Options:
- Use `S (max_list L)`.
- Or use the `Atom` library? Not available.

Let me define:
```
Fixpoint max_list (l : list nat) : nat :=
  match l with
  | [] => 0
  | x :: xs => Nat.max x (max_list xs)
  end.
Lemma max_list_spec : forall l x, In x l -> x <= max_list l.
```
Prove by induction on l; if x = a then a ≤ max a (max_list) ✓; else use IH and Nat.max bounds.

Then fresh L := S (max_list L). `~ In (fresh L) L`: intro H; apply max_list_spec in H; lia (x ≤ m and x = S m contradicts).

For choosing y ∉ L ∪ fv t1: fresh (L ++ fv t1). Then ~ In y L and ~ In y (fv t1) by list lemmas (in_app_or).

Alternatively, avoid fv lists: use a function `fresh_atom (L : list atom) : atom` and prove `~ In`. We still need the union.

But hold on: in R_expand's arrow case, we need `SN t`; derived from all successors R and R_SN. But R_SN at the *same* type arrow is part of the conjunction being proven in the induction. Hmm, within the arrow case, we're proving P4(T1→T2) and we may use P1(T1→T2) which is proven in the same case (the conjunction arms). In Coq we can't easily use one arm while proving another within a single `split`. We can structure the proof as:

```
Lemma R_props : forall T, R_sn T /\ R_lc_prop T /\ R_expand_prop T.
Proof.
  induction T as [| T1 IH1 T2 IH2].
  - (* Bool *) destruct IH... no IH.
    split; [..|split].
    + intros t [Hlc Hsn]; exact Hsn.
    + intros t [Hlc Hsn]; exact Hlc.
    + intros t Hlc Hnonabs Hsucc. split; [exact Hlc|]. apply SN_intro; intros t' Hstep; apply R_SN... 
```
Hmm, in the Bool case, P4's conclusion R Bool t = lc t ∧ SN t; we can construct directly. For SN: `constructor; intros t' Hstep; apply (proj1 ...)` — we need R_SN for Bool, which is the first arm being proven. But we can just unfold R and prove SN directly: `apply SN_intro; intros t' Hs; destruct (Hsucc t' Hs) as [Hlc' Hsn']; exact Hsn'`. ✓ (unfolding R Bool).

For the arrow case, we prove the three arms; while proving P4 arrow, we need P1 at arrow (to convert successors' R to SN) — we can just destruct the successor's R_arrow triple directly: R_arrow t' = lc t' ∧ SN t' ∧ app clause, so `destruct (Hsucc t' Hstep) as [Hlc' [Hsn' Happ']]` gives SN ✓ without needing P1 arrow. Similarly for other uses. So we can avoid intra-arm dependencies by unfolding R at the arrow type directly in the proof.

But in P4 arrow, we need P1/P2/P4 at T1 and T2 (from IH1/IH2) ✓.

Let me write the arrow case of P4 fully:

So the type induction proves four properties:
```
R_props T := 
  (forall t, R T t -> SN t)                        (* P1 *)
  /\ (forall t, R T t -> lc t)                     (* P2 *)
  /\ (forall t t', R T t -> step t t' -> R T t')   (* P3 = CR2 forward *)
  /\ (forall t, lc t -> ~is_abs t -> (forall t', step t t' -> R T t') -> R T t)  (* P4 expand *)
```

**Fundamental theorem:**

```
Lemma fundamental : forall Gamma t T,
  has_type Gamma t T ->
  forall f,
    (forall x, lc_at 0 (f x)) ->
    (forall x T, Gamma x = Some T -> R T (f x)) ->
    R T (subst f t).

  intros Gamma t T Hty.
  induction Hty; intros f Hflc HfR.
  - (* T_Var *) apply HfR. assumption.
  - (* T_Abs *) 
    (* Goal: R (T1 -> T2) (subst f (tm_abs T1 t1)) = R (T1->T2) (tm_abs T1 (subst f t1)) *)
    apply R_abs.
    + (* lc (abs T1 (subst f t1)) *) 
      Need lc_at 1 (subst f t1).
      ...
    + (* beta property *) intros v Hv HRv.
      (* Goal R T2 (open (subst f t1) v) *)
      ...
  - (* T_App *)
    (* Goal R T2 (subst f (tm_app t1 t2)) = R T2 (tm_app (subst f t1) (subst f t2)) *)
    apply R_app... 
```

```
Lemma R_app : forall T1 T2 t1 t2, R (Ty_Arrow T1 T2) t1 -> R T1 t2 -> R T2 (tm_app t1 t2).
Proof. intros. destruct H as [_ [_ Happ]]; apply Happ; assumption. Qed.
```

- T_True/T_False: R Bool tm_true = lc ∧ SN; lc ✓; SN: no steps (true doesn't step: `true_no_step` via inversion) → SN_intro.

Now the details of the T_Abs case.

Context: 
- Hty : forall x, ~ In x L -> has_type (update Gamma x T1) (open t1 (tm_fvar x)) T2.

```
Fixpoint R (T : ty) : tm -> Prop :=
  match T with
  | Ty_Bool => fun t => locally_closed t /\ strongly_normalizing t
  | Ty_Arrow T1 T2 => fun t =>
      locally_closed t /\ strongly_normalizing t /\
      (forall u, R T1 u -> R T2 (tm_app t u))
  end.
```

```coq
(** ** Substitution and free variables *)

Definition extend (f : atom -> tm) (x : atom) (v : tm) : atom -> tm :=
  fun y => if Nat.eqb x y then v else f y.

Fixpoint subst (f : atom -> tm) (t : tm) : tm :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar i => tm_bvar i
  | tm_fvar x => f x
  | tm_app t1 t2 => tm_app (subst f t1) (subst f t2)
  | tm_abs T t1 => tm_abs T (subst f t1)
  | tm_true => tm_true
  | tm_false => tm_false
  end.

Fixpoint fv (t : tm) : list atom :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar _ => nil
  | tm_fvar x => cons x nil
  | tm_app t1 t2 => app (fv t1) (fv t2)
  | tm_abs _ t1 => fv t1
  | tm_true => nil
  | tm_false => nil
  end.

Fixpoint max_list (l : list nat) : nat :=
  match l with
  | nil => 0
  | cons x xs => Nat.max x (max_list xs)
  end.

Definition fresh (l : list atom) : atom := S (max_list l).

Lemma max_list_spec : forall l x, In x l -> x <= max_list l.
Proof.
  induction l as [| a l IH]; intros x Hin.
  - inversion Hin.
  - simpl in Hin. destruct Hin as [-> | Hin].
    + apply Nat.le_max_l.
    + apply Nat.le_trans with (max_list l); [apply IH; assumption | apply Nat.le_max_r].
Qed.

Lemma fresh_spec : forall l, ~ In (fresh l) l.
Proof.
  intros l Hin. unfold fresh in Hin.
  apply max_list_spec in Hin.
  pose proof (Nat.lt_succ_diag_r (max_list l)).
  lia.
Qed.
```
Hmm, `Nat.lt_succ_diag_r` might be named `Nat.lt_succ_diag_r : forall n, n < S n` ✓ in PeanoNat. Or use lia directly: from Hin : S (max_list l) <= max_list l, lia.

Then fresh for a combined list: `fresh (app L (fv t1))`; need `~ In y L` and `~ In y (fv t1)`:
```
Lemma not_in_app_1 : forall (l1 l2 : list atom) x, ~ In x (app l1 l2) -> ~ In x l1.
Proof. intros; intro H; apply H; apply in_or_app; left; assumption. Qed.
Lemma not_in_app_2 : ...
```

Or simply: `apply in_app_or in H`. List provides `in_app_or : forall (A : Type) (l m : list A) (a : A), In a (l ++ m) -> In a l \/ In a m` and `in_or_app`. ✓

```
Proof.
  induction t; intros k k' Hle H; inversion H; subst.
  - apply lc_bvar. lia.
  - apply lc_fvar.
  - apply lc_app; auto.
  - apply lc_abs; [apply IHt with (k := S k) |]; [lia | assumption].
  - apply lc_true.
  - apply lc_false.
```

Hmm, careful: `subst f (open_rec k u (tm_bvar n))`: subst f applied to `(if Nat.eqb k n then u else tm_bvar n)`. `subst` doesn't reduce on an if-then-else (since the term isn't a constructor syntactically). Need to destruct (Nat.eqb k n) first. So: `destruct (Nat.eqb k n) eqn:E; simpl`. Then LHS = subst f u; RHS = open_rec k (subst f u) (tm_bvar n) = if k =? n then subst f u else bvar n; with E : eqb k n = true → RHS = subst f u ✓. And the false case similar.
  Note: after `simpl`, the goal's LHS is subst f (if ... then u else tm_bvar n) and the RHS is (if Nat.eqb k n then subst f u else tm_bvar n). So destruct eqb then simpl both sides.
- fvar x: LHS = subst f (f x); RHS = open_rec k (subst f u) (f x) = f x by open_rec_lc_gen with lc_at 0 (f x) and 0 <= k ✓.
- app: LHS = subst f (app (open_rec k u t1) (open_rec k u t2)) = app (subst f (open_rec k u t1)) (subst f (open_rec k u t2)); rewrite IHt1, IHt2; RHS = open_rec k (subst f u) (app (subst f t1) (subst f t2)) = app (open_rec k ...) (open_rec k ...) ✓.
- abs: LHS = subst f (abs T (open_rec (S k) u body)) = abs T (subst f (open_rec (S k) u body)); IH body with k := S k gives = abs T (open_rec (S k) (subst f u) (subst f body)); RHS = open_rec k (subst f u) (abs T (subst f body)) = abs T (open_rec (S k) (subst f u) (subst f body)) ✓. Need subst (abs T body) = abs T (subst f body) by simpl ✓.

```
Lemma subst_extend_fresh : forall f x v t,

  - rewrite IHt. reflexivity. intro H; apply Hfresh; simpl; assumption.
  - reflexivity.
  - reflexivity.
Qed.
```

`step_lc_back`:

```
Lemma step_lc_back : forall t t', step t t' -> locally_closed t' -> locally_closed t.
Proof.
  intros t t' H. induction H; intros Hlc.
  - (* ST_AppAbs *) apply lc_app; [assumption | apply value_lc; assumption].
  - (* ST_App1 *) apply lc_at_app_inv in Hlc as [Hlc1' Hlc2]; apply lc_app; [apply IHstep; assumption | assumption].
  - (* ST_App2 *) apply lc_at_app_inv in Hlc as [Hlc1 Hlc2']; apply lc_app; [apply value_lc; assumption | apply IHstep; assumption].
Qed.
```

`step_det`:
```
Lemma step_det : forall t t1 t2, step t t1 -> step t t2 -> t1 = t2.
Proof.
  intros t t1 t2 H. induction H; intros H2.
  - (* ST_AppAbs T t0 v Hlc Hv *) inversion H2; subst.
    + reflexivity.
    + (* ST_App1: premise step (tm_abs T t0) t1' *) exfalso; apply abs_no_step in H4? 
```
Hmm, naming. Let me think about how inversion on H2 works here. H2 : step (tm_app (tm_abs T t0) v) t2.
- ST_AppAbs: gives t2 = open t0 v ✓ (unification of the app structure: tm_app (tm_abs T t0) v = tm_app (tm_abs T' t') v' → T=T', t0=t', v=v') → reflexivity.
- ST_App1: premise t1' : step (tm_abs T t0) t1' — contradict abs_no_step.
- ST_App2: premise value (tm_abs T t0) and step v t2' — contradict value_no_step.
- ST_App1 case (first derivation): t = tm_app t1 t2 with Hstep1 : t1 --> t1'; t1' (result) = tm_app t1' t2. H2 : step (tm_app t1 t2) t2_res. Inversion:
  - ST_AppAbs: gives t1 = tm_abs T' t' → then Hstep1 : step (tm_abs T' t') t1' contradicts abs_no_step.
  - ST_App1: gives H2' : t1 --> t1'' and t2_res = tm_app t1'' t2. IHstep : forall t2, step t1 t2 -> t1' = t2? Wait, the IH generated for Hstep1 in `induction H` is: `IHstep : forall t2, step t1 t2 -> t1' = t2`? Since the statement is `forall t1 t2, step t t1 -> step t t2 -> t1 = t2` and we're inducting on H, the IH for the premise `t1 --> t1'` should be the statement with t := t1, t1 := t1', i.e., `IHstep : forall t2, step t1 t2 -> t1' = t2`. Hmm, the induction motive: the goal after `intros t t1 t2 H` is `step t t2 -> t1 = t2`. The motive P := fun (t t1 : tm) (H : step t t1) => forall t2, step t t2 -> t1 = t2. So IHstep : forall t2, step t1 t2 -> t1' = t2 ✓. Then use IHstep with t2 := t1'' to get t1' = t1''; then t2_res = tm_app t1'' t2 = tm_app t1' t2 ✓.
  - ST_App2: gives value t1 and step t2 t2' — contradict Hstep1 : step t1 t1' (value_no_step).
- ST_App2 case: t = tm_app v1 t2, Hval : value v1, Hstep2 : t2 --> t2', t1 (result) = tm_app v1 t2'. H2 : step (tm_app v1 t2) t2_res. Inversion:
  - ST_AppAbs: v1 = tm_abs ... — impossible since v1 is a value? Hmm: ST_AppAbs unifies tm_app v1 t2 with tm_app (tm_abs T t) v — that's satisfiable (v1 could be an abs)! Then the premise is `locally_closed (tm_abs T t)` which is fine, and value v. So ST_AppAbs case gives t2_res = open t v where v1 = tm_abs T t, t2 = v. But we also have Hstep2 : step t2 t2', i.e., step v t2' — contradict value v (from ST_AppAbs's premise value v, which corresponds to t2 here) ✓. So contradiction via value_no_step.

  - ST_App2: gives value v1 and step t2 t2''; IHstep2 : forall t2, step t2 t2 -> t2' = t2; get t2'' = t2' ✓ → t2_res = tm_app v1 t2'' = tm_app v1 t2' ✓.

This is doable with inversion; naming will be fiddly. I'll write it and adjust based on errors.

Let me write the definitions and basic lemmas now.

```coq
(** * Auxiliary definitions and lemmas for the normalization proof. *)

(** ** Substitution of free variables *)

Definition extend (f : atom -> tm) (x : atom) (v : tm) : atom -> tm :=
  fun y => if Nat.eqb x y then v else f y.

Fixpoint subst (f : atom -> tm) (t : tm) : tm :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar i => tm_bvar i
  | tm_fvar x => f x
  | tm_app t1 t2 => tm_app (subst f t1) (subst f t2)
  | tm_abs T t1 => tm_abs T (subst f t1)
  | tm_true => tm_true
  | tm_false => tm_false
  end.

Fixpoint fv (t : tm) : list atom :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar _ => nil
  | tm_fvar x => [x]
  | tm_app t1 t2 => fv t1 ++ fv t2
  | tm_abs _ t1 => fv t1
  | tm_true => nil
  | tm_false => nil
  end.

Fixpoint max_list (l : list nat) : nat :=
  match l with
  | nil => 0
  | x :: xs => Nat.max x (max_list xs)
  end.

Definition fresh (l : list atom) : atom := S (max_list l).

Lemma max_list_spec : forall l x, In x l -> x <= max_list l.
Proof.
  induction l as [| a l IH]; intros x Hin; simpl in *.
  - destruct Hin.
  - destruct Hin as [-> | Hin].
    + apply Nat.le_max_l.
    + apply Nat.le_trans with (max_list l); [apply IH; assumption | apply Nat.le_max_r].
Qed.

Lemma fresh_spec : forall l, ~ In (fresh l) l.
Proof.
  intros l Hin. unfold fresh in Hin.
  apply max_list_spec in Hin.
  pose proof (Nat.lt_succ_diag_r (max_list l)). lia.
Qed.
```

Wait: in max_list_spec's nil case, `destruct Hin` proves False from `In x []`? `simpl in *` turns Hin into `In x nil` which reduces to `False`? Actually `In x []` unfolds to False definitionally? `In` is defined as:
```
Fixpoint In (a:A) (l:list A) : Prop :=
  match l with
  | [] => False
  | b :: m => b = a \/ In a m
  end.
```
So `In x []` reduces to False, and `destruct Hin` closes ✓. After simpl, Hin : False; `destruct Hin` ✓ (or `contradiction`).

```coq
(** ** Basic properties of local closure *)

Lemma lc_at_bvar_inv : forall k i, lc_at k (tm_bvar i) -> i < k.
Proof. intros k i H. inversion H. assumption. Qed.

Lemma lc_at_app_inv : forall k t1 t2,
  lc_at k (tm_app t1 t2) -> lc_at k t1 /\ lc_at k t2.
Proof. intros k t1 t2 H. inversion H. split; assumption. Qed.

Lemma lc_at_abs_inv : forall k T t,
  lc_at k (tm_abs T t) -> lc_at (S k) t.
Proof. intros k T t H. inversion H. assumption. Qed.

Lemma lc_at_mono : forall t k k', k <= k' -> lc_at k t -> lc_at k' t.
Proof.
  intros t. induction t; intros k k' Hle H.
  - apply lc_bvar. apply lc_at_bvar_inv in H. lia.
  - apply lc_fvar.
  - apply lc_at_app_inv in H as [H1 H2]. apply lc_app; [apply IHt1 | apply IHt2]; assumption.
  - apply lc_abs. apply IHt with (k := S k); [lia | apply lc_at_abs_inv in H; assumption].
  - apply lc_true.
  - apply lc_false.
Qed.

Lemma lc_at_open_rec : forall k t, lc_at (S k) t -> forall u,

  - apply lc_at_app_inv in H as [H1 H2]. apply lc_app; [apply IHt1 | apply IHt2]; assumption.
  - apply lc_abs. apply IHt with (k := S k).
    + apply lc_at_abs_inv in H. exact H.
    + apply lc_at_mono with (k := k); [lia | exact Hu].
  - apply lc_true.
  - apply lc_false.
Qed.
```

```
Lemma open_rec_lc_inv : forall t k x,
  lc_at k (open_rec k (tm_fvar x) t) -> lc_at (S k) t.
Proof.
  induction t; intros k y H; simpl in H.

    destruct (Nat.eqb k n) eqn:E; simpl in H.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst n. apply lc_bvar. lia.
```

Will `inversion H as [u Hpred]` work? In the test `inversion H as [t0 Hpred]` worked and gave Hpred : forall t' : nat, st t t' -> sn t' ✓. So yes.

Now, writing the full R_props proof. Let me write it in detail.

```coq
Fixpoint R (T : ty) : tm -> Prop :=
  match T with
  | Ty_Bool => fun t => locally_closed t /\ strongly_normalizing t
  | Ty_Arrow T1 T2 => fun t =>
      locally_closed t /\ strongly_normalizing t /\
      (forall u, R T1 u -> R T2 (tm_app t u))
  end.

Definition is_abs (t : tm) : Prop := exists T body, t = tm_abs T body.

Lemma R_props : forall T,
  (forall t, R T t -> strongly_normalizing t) /\
  (forall t, R T t -> locally_closed t) /\
  (forall t t', R T t -> step t t' -> R T t') /\
  (forall t, locally_closed t -> ~ is_abs t ->
     (forall t', step t t' -> R T t') -> R T t).
Proof.
  induction T as [| T1 IH1 T2 IH2].
  - (* Bool *)
    simpl. split.
    + intros t [Hlc Hsn]. exact Hsn.
    + split.
      * intros t [Hlc Hsn]. exact Hlc.
      * split.
        -- intros t t' [Hlc Hsn] Hstep. split.
           ++ apply step_lc_fwd with (t := t); assumption.
           ++ apply SN_step with (t := t); assumption.
        -- intros t Hlc Hnonabs Hsucc. split; [assumption|].
           apply SN_intro. intros t' Hstep. destruct (Hsucc t' Hstep) as [Hlc' Hsn']. exact Hsn'.
  - (* Arrow *)
    simpl. split.
    + (* P1 *) intros t [_ [Hsn _]]. exact Hsn.
    + split.
      * (* P2 *) intros t [Hlc _]. exact Hlc.
      * split.
        -- (* P3: forward closure *) 
           intros t t' HR Hstep.
           destruct HR as [Hlc [Hsn Happ]].
           split.
           ++ apply step_lc_fwd with (t := t); assumption.
           ++ split.
              ** apply SN_step with (t := t); assumption.
              ** intros u Hu.
                 apply (proj1 (proj2 (proj2 IH2)) (tm_app t u) (tm_app t' u)).
                 (* IH2's P3: forall t t', R T2 t -> step t t' -> R T2 t' *)
                 --- apply Happ; assumption.
                 --- apply ST_App1; [assumption | apply (proj1 (proj2 IH1) u Hu)].
```
Hmm, IH2 is the conjunction for T2: `(forall t, R T2 t -> SN t) /\ (forall t, R T2 t -> lc t) /\ (forall t t', R T2 t -> step t t' -> R T2 t') /\ (...)`. So P3 for T2 is `proj1 (proj2 (proj2 IH2))`. And P2 for T1 is `proj1 (proj2 IH1)`.

This projection style is verbose. Let me instead define named lemmas by destructuring once per IH:

```coq
(** ** Auxiliary definitions and lemmas *)

Definition extend (f : atom -> tm) (x : atom) (v : tm) : atom -> tm :=
  fun y => if Nat.eqb x y then v else f y.

Fixpoint subst (f : atom -> tm) (t : tm) : tm :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar i => tm_bvar i
  | tm_fvar x => f x
  | tm_app t1 t2 => tm_app (subst f t1) (subst f t2)
  | tm_abs T t1 => tm_abs T (subst f t1)
  | tm_true => tm_true
  | tm_false => tm_false
  end.

Fixpoint fv (t : tm) : list atom :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar _ => nil
  | tm_fvar x => [x]
  | tm_app t1 t2 => fv t1 ++ fv t2
  | tm_abs _ t1 => fv t1
  | tm_true => nil
  | tm_false => nil
  end.

Fixpoint max_list (l : list nat) : nat :=
  match l with
  | nil => 0
  | x :: xs => Nat.max x (max_list xs)
  end.

Definition fresh (l : list atom) : atom := S (max_list l).

Lemma max_list_spec : forall (l : list nat) (x : nat), In x l -> x <= max_list l.
Proof.
  induction l as [| a l IH]; intros x Hin; simpl in *.
  - destruct Hin.
  - destruct Hin as [-> | Hin].
    + apply Nat.le_max_l.
    + apply Nat.le_trans with (max_list l); [apply IH; assumption | apply Nat.le_max_r].
Qed.

Lemma fresh_spec : forall (l : list atom), ~ In (fresh l) l.
Proof.
  intros l Hin. unfold fresh in Hin. apply max_list_spec in Hin.
  pose proof (Nat.lt_succ_diag_r (max_list l)). lia.
Qed.

Lemma exists_fresh : forall (l : list atom), exists x, ~ In x l.
Proof. intros l. exists (fresh l). apply fresh_spec. Qed.
```

The block compiled. Now let me continue with the value/step lemmas and has_type_lc, then R.

Let me append to block.v:

```coq
(** *** Values, steps, and local closure *)

Lemma value_lc : forall v, value v -> locally_closed v.
Proof.
  intros v Hv. induction Hv.
  - assumption.
  - apply lc_true.
  - apply lc_false.
Qed.

Lemma abs_no_step : forall (T : ty) (t t' : tm), ~ step (tm_abs T t) t'.
Proof. intros T t t' H. inversion H. Qed.

Lemma true_no_step : forall t', ~ step tm_true t'.
Proof. intros t' H. inversion H. Qed.

Lemma false_no_step : forall t', ~ step tm_false t'.
Proof. intros t' H. inversion H. Qed.

Lemma value_no_step : forall v t', value v -> ~ step v t'.
Proof. intros v t' Hv Hstep. destruct Hv; inversion Hstep. Qed.

Lemma step_lc_fwd : forall t t', step t t' -> locally_closed t -> locally_closed t'.
Proof.
  intros t t' H. induction H; intros Hlc.
  - apply lc_at_app_inv in Hlc as [Habs Hv].
    apply lc_at_open_rec with (k := 0).
    + apply lc_at_abs_inv in Habs. exact Habs.
    + apply value_lc. assumption.
  - apply lc_at_app_inv in Hlc as [Hlc1 Hlc2]. apply lc_app.
    + apply IHstep. assumption.
    + assumption.
  - apply lc_at_app_inv in Hlc as [Hv1 Hlc2]. apply lc_app.
    + apply value_lc. assumption.
    + apply IHstep. assumption.
Qed.
```

```coq
Lemma step_app_inv : forall t1 t2 s,
  step (tm_app t1 t2) s ->
     (exists T body v, t1 = tm_abs T body /\ t2 = v /\ s = open body v
                       /\ locally_closed (tm_abs T body) /\ value v)
  \/ (exists t1', step t1 t1' /\ s = tm_app t1' t2 /\ locally_closed t2)
  \/ (exists v1 t2', t1 = v1 /\ value v1 /\ step t2 t2' /\ s = tm_app v1 t2').
Proof.
  intros t1 t2 s H. inversion H; subst; eauto 10.
Qed.
```

Then step_det:
```coq
Lemma step_det : forall t t1 t2, step t t1 -> step t t2 -> t1 = t2.
Proof.
  intros t t1 t2 H. induction H; intros H2;
    inversion H2; subst;
    try reflexivity;
    try (exfalso; eapply abs_no_step; eassumption);
    try (exfalso; eapply value_no_step; eassumption);
    try (f_equal; apply IHstep; eassumption).
Qed.
```

```
Lemma step_det : forall t t1 t2, step t t1 -> step t t2 -> t1 = t2.
Proof.
  intros t t1 t2 H. induction H; intros H2.
  - (* ST_AppAbs *)
    inversion H2; subst.
    + reflexivity.
    + exfalso. eapply abs_no_step. eassumption.
    + exfalso. eapply value_step_absurd; eassumption.
  - (* ST_App1 *)
    inversion H2; subst.
    + exfalso. eapply abs_no_step. eassumption.

    + exfalso. eapply value_step_absurd; eassumption.

    + f_equal. apply IHstep. eassumption.
```

```coq
(** *** Typing and local closure *)

Lemma has_type_lc : forall Gamma t T, has_type Gamma t T -> locally_closed t.
Proof.
  intros Gamma t T H. induction H.
  - apply lc_fvar.
  - destruct (exists_fresh L) as [y Hy].
    apply lc_abs. apply open_rec_lc_inv with (k := 0) (x := y).
    apply IHH. assumption.
  - apply lc_at_app_inv? no — the goal is lc (app t1 t2); the IHs give lc t1, lc t2.
    apply lc_app; assumption.
  - apply lc_true.
  - apply lc_false.
Qed.
```

Now the R definitions and R_props. Let me write them.

```coq
(** *** The logical relation *)

Fixpoint R (T : ty) : tm -> Prop :=
  match T with
  | Ty_Bool => fun t => locally_closed t /\ strongly_normalizing t
  | Ty_Arrow T1 T2 => fun t =>
      locally_closed t /\ strongly_normalizing t /\
      (forall u, R T1 u -> R T2 (tm_app t u))
  end.

Definition is_abs (t : tm) : Prop := exists T body, t = tm_abs T body.
```

Now R_props. Let me write it carefully with the projection names.

```coq
Lemma R_props : forall T,
  (forall t, R T t -> strongly_normalizing t) /\
  (forall t, R T t -> locally_closed t) /\
  (forall t t', R T t -> step t t' -> R T t') /\
  (forall t, locally_closed t -> ~ is_abs t ->
     (forall t', step t t' -> R T t') -> R T t).
Proof.
  induction T as [| T1 IH1 T2 IH2].
  - (* Bool *)
    simpl. split.
    + intros t [Hlc Hsn]. exact Hsn.
    + split.
      * intros t [Hlc Hsn]. exact Hlc.
      * split.
        -- intros t t' [Hlc Hsn] Hstep. split.
           ++ apply step_lc_fwd with (t := t); assumption.
           ++ apply SN_step with (t := t); assumption.
        -- intros t Hlc Hnonabs Hsucc. split; [assumption|].
           apply SN_intro. intros t' Hstep. destruct (Hsucc t' Hstep) as [Hlc' Hsn']. exact Hsn'.
  - (* Arrow *)
    destruct IH1 as [IH1_sn [IH1_lc [IH1_fwd IH1_exp]]].
    destruct IH2 as [IH2_sn [IH2_lc [IH2_fwd IH2_exp]]].
    split.
    + (* P1 *) intros t HR. simpl in HR. destruct HR as [Hlc [Hsn Happ]]. exact Hsn.
    + split.
      * (* P2 *) intros t HR. simpl in HR. destruct HR as [Hlc _]. exact Hlc.
      * split.
        -- (* P3 forward closure *)
           intros t t' HR Hstep. simpl in HR. destruct HR as [Hlc [Hsn Happ]].
           simpl. split.
           ++ apply step_lc_fwd with (t := t); assumption.
           ++ split.
              ** apply SN_step with (t := t); assumption.
              ** intros u Hu. apply IH2_fwd with (t := tm_app t u).
                 --- apply Happ. assumption.
                 --- apply ST_App1. 
                     +++ assumption.
                     +++ apply IH1_lc. assumption.
        -- (* P4 expand for non-abstractions *)
           intros t Hlc Hnonabs Hsucc. simpl. split; [assumption|]. split.
           ++ apply SN_intro. intros t' Hstep. destruct (Hsucc t' Hstep) as [_ [Hsn' _]]. exact Hsn'.
           ++ intros u Hu.
              assert (Haux : forall u, strongly_normalizing u -> R T1 u -> R T2 (tm_app t u)).
              { intros u0 Hsn. induction Hsn as [u0 Hsuccu IHu]. intros Hu0.
                apply IH2_exp with (t := tm_app t u0).
                - simpl. split; [exact Hlc | apply IH1_lc; exact Hu0].
                - intros [T' [body Heq]]. discriminate Heq.
                - intros s Hs. apply step_app_inv in Hs.
                  destruct Hs as [[T' [body [v [Heq_t [Heq_u [Heq_s [Hlcv Hval]]]]]]]
                               | [[t1' [Hstep1 [Heq_s Hlc2]]] | [v1 [u' [Heq_t [Hval [Hstepu Heq_s]]]]]]].
                  + (* beta: impossible since t is not an abs *)
                    exfalso. apply Hnonabs. exists T', body. exact Heq_t.
                  + (* app1: use Hsucc *)
                    subst s. destruct (Hsucc t1' Hstep1) as [_ [_ Happ']]. apply Happ'. exact Hu0.
                  + (* app2: use IH *)
                    subst s. subst v1. apply IHu; [exact Hstepu | apply IH1_fwd with (t := u0); assumption].
              }
              apply Haux; [apply IH1_sn; exact Hu | exact Hu].
Qed.
```

```coq
Lemma R_abs : forall T1 T2 body,
  locally_closed (tm_abs T1 body) ->
  (forall v, value v -> R T1 v -> R T2 (open body v)) ->
  R (Ty_Arrow T1 T2) (tm_abs T1 body).

  - intros u Hu. apply (R_abs_app_aux u).
    + apply (R_SN T1 u Hu).
    + exact Hlc.
    + exact Hbeta.
    + exact Hu.
Qed.
```

Actually, a cleaner robust approach: use `induction H as [...]` with explicit names. Let me figure out the pattern:

Constructors:
1. T_Var : forall Gamma x T, Gamma x = Some T -> has_type Gamma (tm_fvar x) T.
2. T_Abs : forall (L : list atom) Gamma T1 T2 t1, (forall x, ~ In x L -> has_type (update Gamma x T1) (open t1 (tm_fvar x)) T2) -> has_type Gamma (tm_abs T1 t1) (Ty_Arrow T1 T2).
3. T_App : forall Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2, has_type Gamma t1 (Ty_Arrow T1 T2) -> has_type Gamma t2 T1 -> has_type Gamma (tm_app t1 t2) T2.
4. T_True : forall Gamma, has_type Gamma tm_true Ty_Bool.
5. T_False : forall Gamma, has_type Gamma tm_false Ty_Bool.

With Coq's `induction H as [pat1 | pat2 | pat3 | pat4 | pat5]`, each pattern binds the constructor's arguments. For arguments that are determined by the indices (like Gamma, t, T in T_Var), Coq usually requires names anyway. The number of names must match the number of arguments (including non-dependent ones?). In practice, for an inductive family, `induction H as [x T Hctx | L Gamma T1 T2 t1 IH | ...]` might fail if Coq expects binders for the indices too.

Now let me write the full remaining block:

```coq
Lemma subst_open_fresh : forall f v y t,
  (forall x, lc_at 0 (f x)) ->
  lc_at 0 v ->
  ~ In y (fv t) ->
  subst (extend f y v) (open t (tm_fvar y)) = open (subst f t) v.
Proof.
  intros f v y t Hf Hv Hy.
  assert (Hf' : forall x, lc_at 0 (extend f y v x)).
  { intros x. unfold extend. destruct (Nat.eqb y x); [exact Hv | apply Hf]. }
  rewrite (subst_open_rec t (extend f y v) Hf' 0 (tm_fvar y)).
  change (open_rec 0 (if Nat.eqb y y then v else f y) (subst (extend f y v) t) = open_rec 0 v (subst f t)).
  rewrite Nat.eqb_refl. cbn.
  rewrite (subst_extend_fresh t f y v Hy). reflexivity.
Qed.
```

```coq
Lemma R_SN : forall T t, R T t -> strongly_normalizing t.
Proof. intros T. exact (proj1 (R_props T)). Qed.

Lemma R_lc : forall T t, R T t -> locally_closed t.
Proof. intros T. exact (proj1 (proj2 (R_props T))). Qed.

Lemma R_fwd : forall T t t', R T t -> step t t' -> R T t'.
Proof. intros T. exact (proj1 (proj2 (proj2 (R_props T)))). Qed.

Lemma R_expand : forall T t, locally_closed t -> ~ is_abs t ->
  (forall t', step t t' -> R T t') -> R T t.
Proof. intros T. exact (proj2 (proj2 (proj2 (R_props T)))). Qed.

Lemma R_step : forall T t t', R T t' -> step t t' -> R T t.
Proof.
  intros T t t' HR Hstep.
  apply R_expand.
  - apply step_lc_back with (t' := t').
    + exact Hstep.
    + exact (R_lc T t' HR).
  - intros [T' [body Heq]]. subst t. exact (abs_no_step T' body t' Hstep).
  - intros s Hs. rewrite (step_det t s t' Hs Hstep). exact HR.
Qed.

Lemma R_app : forall T1 T2 t1 t2,
  R (Ty_Arrow T1 T2) t1 -> R T1 t2 -> R T2 (tm_app t1 t2).
Proof.
  intros T1 T2 t1 t2 HR Hu. simpl in HR.
  destruct HR as [_ [_ Happ]]. apply Happ. exact Hu.
Qed.
```

```coq
Lemma R_abs_app_aux : forall u,
  strongly_normalizing u ->
  forall T1 T2 body,
    locally_closed (tm_abs T1 body) ->
    (forall v, value v -> R T1 v -> R T2 (open body v)) ->
    R T1 u ->
    R T2 (tm_app (tm_abs T1 body) u).
```

```coq
Lemma R_abs : forall T1 T2 body,
  locally_closed (tm_abs T1 body) ->
  (forall v, value v -> R T1 v -> R T2 (open body v)) ->
  R (Ty_Arrow T1 T2) (tm_abs T1 body).

  - intros u Hu. apply (R_abs_app_aux u).

    + exact Hu.
Qed.
```

```coq
Lemma fundamental : forall Gamma t T,
  has_type Gamma t T ->
  forall f,
    (forall x, lc_at 0 (f x)) ->
    (forall x T0, Gamma x = Some T0 -> R T0 (f x)) ->
    R T (subst f t).
Proof.
  intros Gamma t T H. induction H; intros f Hflc HfR.
  - (* T_Var *) apply HfR. exact H.
  - (* T_Abs *) simpl. apply R_abs.
    + destruct (exists_fresh (L ++ fv t1)) as [y Hy].
      assert (HyL : ~ In y L) by (intro Hc; apply Hy; apply in_or_app; now left).
      assert (Hyfv : ~ In y (fv t1)) by (intro Hc; apply Hy; apply in_or_app; now right).
      apply lc_abs.
      assert (Hty : has_type (update Gamma y T1) (open t1 (tm_fvar y)) T2) by (apply H; exact HyL).
      apply has_type_lc in Hty.
      apply open_rec_lc_inv with (k := 0) in Hty.
      apply lc_subst with (k := 1).
      * intros x. apply lc_at_mono with (k := 0); [lia | apply Hflc].
      * exact Hty.
    + intros v Hv HRv.
      destruct (exists_fresh (L ++ fv t1)) as [y Hy].
      assert (HyL : ~ In y L) by (intro Hc; apply Hy; apply in_or_app; now left).
      assert (Hyfv : ~ In y (fv t1)) by (intro Hc; apply Hy; apply in_or_app; now right).
      assert (Hf'lc : forall x, lc_at 0 (extend f y v x)).
      { intros x. unfold extend. destruct (Nat.eqb y x); [apply value_lc; exact Hv | apply Hflc]. }
      assert (Hf'R : forall x T0, update Gamma y T1 x = Some T0 -> R T0 (extend f y v x)).
      { intros x T0 Hctx. unfold update in Hctx.
        destruct (Nat.eqb y x) eqn:E.
        - apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst x. injection Hctx as HTeq. subst T0.
          unfold extend. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl. simpl. exact HRv.
        - apply Nat.eqb_neq in E. unfold extend. rewrite E. simpl.
          apply HfR. exact Hctx. }
      pose proof (H0 y HyL (extend f y v) Hf'lc Hf'R) as Hres.
      rewrite <- subst_open_fresh with (f := f) (v := v) (y := y) (t := t1); [exact Hres | exact Hflc | apply value_lc; exact Hv | exact Hyfv].
  - (* T_App *) simpl. apply R_app.
    + apply IHhas_type1; assumption.
    + apply IHhas_type2; assumption.
  - (* T_True *) simpl. split.
    + apply lc_true.
    + apply SN_intro. intros t' Hstep. inversion Hstep.
  - (* T_False *) simpl. split.
    + apply lc_false.
    + apply SN_intro. intros t' Hstep. inversion Hstep.
Qed.
```

The proof structure:
1. **Substitution & free variables**: `extend`, `subst`, `fv`, `max_list`, `fresh`.
2. **Local closure metatheory**: monotonicity, opening/substitution lemmas, `lc_subst`, `subst_open_rec`, `subst_extend_fresh`, `subst_id`.
3. **Step metatheory**: values don't step, abs don't step, step preserves local closure forward and backward, step inversion for applications, determinism.
4. **Typing**: `has_type_lc`.
5. **Logical relation** `R : ty -> tm -> Prop`:
   - `R Bool t := lc t ∧ SN t`
   - `R (T1→T2) t := lc t ∧ SN t ∧ ∀u. R T1 u → R T2 (t u)`
6. **R_props** by induction on the type: R implies SN, R implies lc, forward closure, backward closure (expand) for non-abstractions.
7. Derived: `R_step` (backward reduction closure), `R_app` (application closure), `R_abs_app_aux` (SN-induction showing an abstraction applied to any reducible argument is reducible), `R_abs` (abstraction introduction).
8. **Fundamental theorem**: `has_type Gamma t T -> forall f, lc(f) -> (∀x T, Gamma x = Some T -> R T (f x)) -> R T (subst f t)`.
9. **normalization**: instantiate the fundamental theorem at the empty context with the identity substitution.
